## francoisgn · 2026-11-01T22:33:39Z

## Report: cloud/legacy-duo

**PR:** #372 `legacy/duo-inner-cover` → `master` (not a draft, not merged)
**Mac check:** #373 (`host: mac`, `type: chore`, `area: app`)

### From #4 (moust4ki): cherry-picked, author kept
| #4 | #372 | Content |
|---|---|---|
| 2cda660 | 796b34b | Free orientation on the inner display, GIFs decoded with the view's `displayScale`, reply button driven by size class. The `sidebarAdaptable` hunk was removed. |
| dbcdc83 | f026682 | Stable refresh buttons (Liquid Glass bubble). |
| c6547fd | e1fdb57 | iOS 27.1 hang workaround (inline titles). |
| b1f07e2 | not carried over | `master` fixed the iOS 27 tab drift differently since (HFR-092). |

Why #4 was not taken as is: the split view stays iPad-only and the open Duo gets a `sidebarAdaptable` `TabView`, which is not the iPad UI that G.1 asks for. It also sits 44 commits behind `master` (base `2440d79`) and conflicts with it.

### New
- 🟢 G.1: `usesSidebarRoot` = regular width AND (regular height OR iPad), so the open Duo gets the HFR-006 split view in portrait and landscape. iPad rule unchanged. Pro Max in landscape keeps the tab bar.
- 🟢 G.2: `HFRswift/Info.plist` is already compliant (`UILaunchScreen`, scenes, no `UIRequiresFullScreen`), so no change was needed. Tab bar on the cover screen.
- 🟢 Inline titles extended to the split columns (environment flag, because the columns are compact). Orientation locks re-applied on fold. `.allButUpsideDown` on the inner display. GIF scale fallback. Edit menu anchored to the view, not the screen.
- 🟢 Changelog: `backlog.md` › « Livrees 4.0.3 », HFR-094 « confirmation que TWO est un clown » (the legacy app has no in-app changelog; `push_realease.md` only holds the release procedure).
- Adversarial review by Fable (another model): no compile blockers found. Findings 4–8 are folded in, 1–3 are listed below as risks.

### Open risks
- 🔴 **Fold/unfold switches the root** (TabView ↔ split view). The open topic and the reading position are not carried over, and the Catégories/Bookmarks/AQ view models reload. With the composer open, the draft is kept, but a post sent after folding does not refresh the new topic view. Same behaviour as an iPad Split View resize, but folding is the main gesture on a Duo.
- 🟠 The rotation setting is ignored on the inner display (based on #4's device observation). To confirm in #373.
- 🟠 Narrow detail in the inner display's portrait with the three-column `.balanced` split.
- 🟠 Nothing built (no Xcode). SwiftLint is not available here.

### Decisions needed (operator)
1. **Fold continuity**: accept the root switch (simple, consistent with the iPad), or fund a follow-up: (a) bridge compact navigation to the split coordinator plus move the per-tab view models up, or (b) a single split root on the Duo, collapsed when folded (no tab bar on the cover screen).
2. Changelog in `backlog.md` as HFR-094: confirm the location and the 4.0.3 version, or name another place.
3. After #373: should the open Duo hide the sidebar by default in portrait (`.prominentDetail` or detail-only)?
4. Should #4 be closed in favour of #372? That is for moust4ki or the operator to decide; I posted only one note on #4.

Temporary files and clones deleted.

## francoisgn · 2026-11-01T22:54:24Z

## Report: cloud/gap-analysis

**G.3 done.** PR #374, squash-merged into `dev-swift` as `dd92bbe`. It refs #368, which stays open for the other workstreams.

- [`docs/migration/GAP-LEGACY-VS-SWIFT.md`](https://github.com/ezzz/HFRnow/blob/dev-swift/docs/migration/GAP-LEGACY-VS-SWIFT.md): 155 rows with stable ids, 12 areas, legacy citations at `2440d79`.
- Images at @3x, split in two because one would be over 15 000 px tall: [iPhone 16 / 18 Pro Max](https://github.com/ezzz/HFRnow/blob/dev-swift/docs/assets/gap-legacy-vs-swift-1-iphone.png) · [iPhone Duo](https://github.com/ezzz/HFRnow/blob/dev-swift/docs/assets/gap-legacy-vs-swift-2-duo.png). Source and Playwright script in `docs/assets/gap/`.
- Citation check: a Sonnet agent checked 20 sampled rows. 10 were exact, 9 had an imprecise line range and 1 was wrong (GAP-056); all 10 are fixed. The report warns that the other ranges may be approximate.
- CodeRabbit raised 5 findings. I fixed 3 and declined 2, each with a reply on its thread:
  - remove the PNGs: the directive asks for them;
  - Playwright counts as a new dependency: `render.js` is an optional doc helper that no manifest, CI job or build loads. **The maintainer may want to confirm this one.**

### Counts per gap type
| Gap | Rows |
|---|---|
| same | 36 |
| ported with changes | 41 |
| UX change | 39 |
| new | 29 |
| not planned | 9 |
| dropped | 1 |

### Top 10 UX changes for the convergence review (H.1)
1. **Legacy oracle revision.** LOT-119 and LOT-170 cite `master`, which has the start screen, the default browser setting and the sectioned iPad sidebar. `2440d79` has none of them (GAP-002, 150, 211).
2. **Topic row.** The legacy Favoris row is the density reference (LOT-169), but LOT-082's row drops "⚑ cur / max", the unread capsule and the flag tints (GAP-026, 027).
3. **Catégories.** A flat list with a sub-forum picker becomes collapsible sections, and the Tous / Favoris / Suivis / Lus filter disappears (GAP-020, 022, 023).
4. **Reader message header.** The grey band with a "+" button becomes a one-line header with a menu (GAP-051, 064).
5. **Reader chrome.** Legacy hides the tab bar. The final reader keeps it and adds a page bar, a scrubber and a quick-reply bar; decide what is default and what is opt-in (GAP-008, 060, 062, 073).
6. **Swipes.** One uniform set with "Lu"/"Annuler". The legacy page-jump context menu is not named in any spec (GAP-028, 029, 034).
7. **Images and videos.** Policy C (Wi-Fi auto, cellular on tap) and tap-to-play videos replace "always load" (GAP-056, 058).
8. **Text size.** A 4-step picker becomes a slider on Dynamic Type; the legacy values need a mapping (GAP-053, 200).
9. **Composer.** Per-topic drafts that block swipe-down, emoji refused at send, first-post delete allowed, a quote tray (GAP-083, 092, 095, 097).
10. **Duo and wide layouts.** The rail, the column widths and the inner-screen portrait layout all wait on the Phase 5 design document, which LOT-147 cannot start without (GAP-212, 214–216).

Also in the report: 9 legacy features with no lot (tab reselect to refresh, quick actions, clear / undo / redo, "+" modes, quote splitting, time-based theme…). And 6 lots labelled "legacy parity" have no source at `2440d79`: MP mark unread, MP delete, MP members, poll creation, multi-window, archived drafts.

### Assumptions (Duo sizes)
- Neither LOT-147 / #237 nor PR #4 gives a size. PR #4 only says the inner screen is regular/regular with a `.phone` idiom.
- I used the published specs as reported by the press: inner screen 7.6 in, 2670 × 1878 px; cover screen 5.4 in, 1398 × 2034 px.
- At an assumed @3x scale, that gives an inner screen of **890 × 626 pt** unfolded (626 × 890 rotated) and a cover screen of **466 × 678 pt**. To check against Apple's developer device sizes.

## francoisgn · 2026-11-02T08:53:37Z

## Operating rules (cloud/orch), v1 2026-11-02 — the latest comment with this title wins

### Operator decisions (2026-11-02, after the night's token review)
- **No PR event subscriptions for the orchestrator.** Each author follows its own PRs. The orchestrator works in **scheduled passes**:
  - every 2 h from 10:07 to 20:07 Europe/Paris (trig_014fchMqipZfowi2ZGx3oXod);
  - hourly from 22:07 to 09:07 (trig_01BmfKm9hCWa4fwT53j7AUw7).
- **Keep the context small.** Read only what the checklist needs, use `fields` and `minimal_output`, and filter REST output with python; never dump JSON. The state lives here, not in the session.
- **Models: downgrade only when quality is not lost.**
  - Sonnet for well-specified volume work (classification, docs, routine fixes, non-risky reviews).
  - Opus for arbitration, design, and risky flows (session, cookies, posting, deletion).
  - Fable for reviewing Opus work and for security.
  - The reviewer is never the author's model (AGENTS.md §6).
- **Parallelism is welcome.** Peaks are acceptable: finishing in 2 days beats 10. Launch as many child sessions as the backlog justifies; each prompt states its scope and its end condition.
- Archive every child as soon as its report is on #368. Prefer a fresh session per scheduled fire over waking a large standing session.

- **Milestones (operator 2026-11-02):** M4 = validated legacy parity; M5 = evolutions. Any change from the initial plan or from legacy goes to M5. Every UXD row follows its recommendation (#379).

- **Steward (operator 2026-11-02: cloud/orch decides):** replaced by a fresh standing v2 session (a fresh-session-per-fire task carries no repository or connector, so it cannot work). Replace it again when its context passes ~300k.
- LOT-161 #251: the version/build and signing questions belong to ezzz (asked on #251).

- **FREEZE (operator 2026-11-02 ~21:45 UTC):** no new child sessions and no new issues. Finish what is in flight (#412, #442, #444, #448, `cloud/legacy-ux`) and keep the tokens for unblocking at the end of the week. In passes, step 3 (New work) is suspended until the operator lifts the freeze. Re-running cancelled CI jobs, merging, closing and steering existing children are still allowed.

- **Steward authority (operator 2026-11-03):** `cloud/docs-steward` inherits the operator's explicit authorization to rewrite the board section of the bodies of #166 and #324 (with a backup and a re-read), and to keep the README and other generated doc tables current through self-merged docs PRs. It never edits text by anyone other than the operator's account.
- **Legacy HFR-096 (operator 2026-11-03):** #372 keeps the split view and #450 tries the tab bar. Bybifok compares them, then either #372 alone or #372 followed by #450 is merged (ezzz merges).
- Freeze exceptions granted by the operator: #452 (move `send(since:)` to `Posting/`) and the #450 tab-bar rework.

- **M2 focus (operator 2026-11-05):**
  - The freeze is lifted for M2 only.
  - App lots labelled `host: mac` **may be coded in the cloud**: CI builds the app, and the PR ends with a "Mac check needed" list.
  - The Mac does only what only the Mac can do: captures, device checks, Mac-claimed lots (prompt `mac/m2-lead`).
  - At most 4 cloud children at a time.
- **Reviews (operator 2026-11-05):** at most 3 rounds. Fable is used only for security, ADRs or ezzz's needs, and for at most 2 rounds.
- **Milestones (operator 2026-11-05):** the first beta ships when M4 closes. LOT-161 moves to M4, so M1 is done.
- **M2 children (cloud/orch, 2026-11-05):**
  - Cloud children cannot wait for CI (they cannot poll), and their merges get denied.
  - So a child pushes, reports on #368 with its head sha and the verdict of each round, and stops.
  - cloud/orch then checks CI, reads everything after the last APPROVE (that read stands in for a 4th round), resolves conflicts and doc nits, merges, and closes the issue.
  - A CI failure that comes only from the flaky FilteredPosts tests (#467) is re-run once.
  - CI since #478 (2026-11-05): CodeQL Swift runs on dev-swift and on PRs labelled `codeql` only (put the label on session, cookie, token or posting PRs). "App build + tests" is required once ezzz re-imports the ruleset. Children **open their PR as a draft** and mark it ready on their last push: drafts fail the macOS checks at once on a Linux gate (red is expected), and `ready_for_review` runs them for real.

- **FREEZE 2 and WATCH-ONLY lifted (operator 2026-11-09, weekly quota reset):** cloud/orch is back in full orchestration, with day passes every 2 h from 10:07 to 20:07 and night passes at 22:07, 01:07, 04:07 and 07:07 Paris (same triggers, same session). Step 3 (New work) runs again, at most 4 children. `ext/francoisgn-suborch-1` drains its pipeline: it finishes what it launched and starts nothing new, then cloud/orch takes over its handed PRs.
- **M5 stays closed (operator 2026-11-09)** until an "M4" build is compiled; no M5 lot is launched.
- **Priority queue:** M2 leftovers, M3, then M4 (#545 glass, #693 behaviour consistency, #572 visual); legacy #372 Duo beta feedback (child `cloud/legacy-duo-feedback`).

### Orchestrator pass (the checklist a scheduled fire runs)
1. **Open PRs** (REST `pulls?state=open`, print only number, base, author, draft, head sha). For each non-draft PR on `dev-swift`:
   - read its head check runs (failures only) and its last 2 non-bot comments;
   - **merge-ready** = CI green + an adversarial APPROVE naming the current head (a merge-only head counts) + no open thread;
   - merge it yourself (squash, `expectedHeadSha`) when AGENTS.md §8 gives the merge to the orchestrator, or when the author's session has finished;
   - then close its `Closes #N` issue with `Done in #PR (merged as <sha>)` and remove its `status:*` labels (operator 2026-11-06);
   - red CI on a PR whose author has finished → fix it if the fix is small, else comment what fails;
   - never touch `master` PRs (ezzz merges them).
2. **Children** (`get_session` on each id listed in the latest Children table here): completed and reported → archive. Stalled for more than 2 h, or looping → steer or interrupt.

## francoisgn · 2026-11-02T22:06:01Z

## Child report cloud/legacy-ux

**Draft PR : #450** (`legacy/ux-bybifok` → `legacy/duo-inner-cover`, sur #372 à `2fe43da`). C'est une évaluation du retour UI de Bybifok (moust4ki), accompagnée de 4 corrections S. Revue adversariale Fable : **APPROVE** au 2e tour. Les deux points majeurs du 1er tour sont corrigés : la mise en avant ne dépend plus du scroll, et le symbole filtre n'est plus réutilisé. Rien n'a été compilé.

### Les 3 verdicts clés
1. 🟠 **Sections dans un menu qui recouvre la liste : vrai en partie.** Sur l'iPhone et l'écran extérieur, il y a déjà une barre d'onglets. Le constat ne vaut que pour la racine split (iPad, Duo ouvert). La HIG (« Consider using a tab bar first », Duo : « Keep controls consistent across device poses ») va dans le sens d'un `TabView` `sidebarAdaptable`, avec liste et sujet côte à côte dans chaque onglet. Cela revient sur le choix de #372, pour un coût L : iOS 18 minimum, reprise de HFR-092, racine iPad. **Non fait**, noté **HFR-096, à décider par ezzz** pour 4.1. Liste et sujet côte à côte sur le Duo ouvert : #372 suit déjà la recommandation Apple (Mail).
2. 🟢 **Actualiser est le bouton le plus voyant, pas Répondre : d'accord, corrigé.** Répondre est désormais le seul bouton mis en avant dans la barre du sujet, sans dépendre du scroll. Compromis : le repère de fin de page HFR-008 est réduit.
3. 🔴 **Favoris en double : pas d'accord.** L'onglet regroupe tous les favoris. Le segment « Favoris » d'une catégorie est le filtre de ce forum (`TopicListFlag.favorites`). Rien n'est retiré.

Corrigés aussi : symboles distincts (Nouveau sujet `plus.bubble`, Répondre en mode forum `arrowshape.turn.up.left`, le `+` est gardé), « Masquer la liste » n'imite plus le bouton de barre latérale, et le bouton des sections de Favoris devient un `Menu` système. Backlog : « Livrees 4.0.5 » HFR-095 (avec le crédit), et HFR-096.

### Vérifications Mac nécessaires (détail dans #450)
- Build `HFRswift` avec Xcode 26.6 (comme la CI) et 27.2 ; tests `TopicBottomBarEmphasisTests` 3/3 et `FoldHandoffStoreTests` 6/6.
- Sujet : Répondre est le seul bouton mis en avant ; Actualiser fonctionne toujours en bas de la dernière page ; pas de clignotement quand le scroll rebondit ; mode recherche et réglage « Pas d'icône ».
- Symboles et VoiceOver ; « Masquer la liste » sur iPad et sur le Duo ouvert ; tap et appui long sur le bouton des sections de Favoris.
- Disponibilité des API citées dans « Pas fait » (`ToolbarOverflowMenu` et `ToolbarItemVisibilityPriority` : iOS 27 ; `buildLimitedAvailability` : iOS 17.5).
- Non-régression de #372 : pliage et dépliage sur un Duo physique (#373 §4).

- **Cause (from code reading, not reproduced; job logs not read):** the split tests' private `Gate` was already signal-based and cannot hang (`select(.group)` always reaches page 2 with the fixture). What failed was `.timeLimit(.minutes(1))`: app tests share the main actor, so on a loaded simulator the limit measures the queue (same lesson as #366; neighbouring tests took 60–100 s; #468 hit it too). Also found a real race in the neighbouring `MessageListReadStateTests`: `waitForRequests` polled with a bounded 2.5 s `Task.sleep` and then went on whether or not the load had started.
- **Fix:** both split tests drop the per-test limit and use the existing `CheckGate` (fail-fast via `entered()` instead of a hang); the read-state tests replace the sleep poll and their private `Gate` with `CheckGate`; both suites get a 5 min `@Suite` limit as a hang backstop only. No assertion weakened.
- **Round 1 (Opus 5.5, on `63b110b`):** APPROVE, posted verbatim on #517. Findings 1 (backstop) and 2 (release `slow` on failed wait) applied in `195d3bb`; 4 (PR text) applied; 3 (`#require` idiom, nit) not applied; 5 (other suites that spin or sleep: `ContentLoaderTests:12`, `PseudoListsSnapshotTests:118`, `ReplyComposerDraftTests:99,215`, `ReplyComposerSendTests:133`) left for a follow-up issue. ## francoisgn · 2026-11-06T12:43:04Z

**Operator decision (2026-11-06 ~12:10 UTC): a closed issue carries no `status:` label.**
- Found: #75, #76, #93 and #97 are closed but still `status: ready`. A scan finds **104 closed issues with a stale status label**: 64 `claimed`, 36 `ready`, 3 `needs-triage`, 1 `blocked`.
- Cause: no rule says to drop the label on closure (ORCHESTRATION only covers the close comment), and `hygiene.py` looks only at open issues. So it is a gap in the rule and the tool, not only a missed sweep.
- Rule from now on:
  - whoever closes an issue removes its `status:*` labels in the same step (cloud/orch, the sub-orchestrator, Mac sessions);
  - `hygiene.py --apply` removes the ones left behind as a routine action, and reports how many it removed.
- `cloud/docs-steward`:
  - (1) adds that routine to `hygiene.py`, with a test, in a self-merged tooling PR;
  - (2) runs it once over the 104 issues (dry run first, list kept in the PR);
  - (3) adds the rule to `docs/agents/ORCHESTRATION.md` next to "Done = …".

PR #543 (LOT-172 part 2, #311): head `a45165f1c589ca3b2b0d1989a73f98b4498409fa`.

**Fixed (a45165f)**
- Quota refusal on a paused sync (CodeRabbit Major, `SyncedListsStore.swift`): confirmed. While the device file could not be saved, a quota refusal ran `catchUp()` first. Its recovery `reconcile()` then replaced the local edit with the older cloud value before `refused(_:)` marked the key. `handle(_:)` now marks refused keys in memory (`markRefused`) before `catchUp()`.
- Paused-edit rule in the spec (CodeRabbit Minor, LOT-172 spec line 88): the rule now depends on the cause of the pause.
  - Owner switch: the digests are cleared, so the cloud list wins, unless a digest came back from the quarantine.
  - Failed save after an ordinary push or apply: the digest kept in memory lets the edit be pushed on recovery.
- Security residual (a), an operation authorized for Apple ID A that resumes after the store switched to B: fixed with an owner check, no redesign. `ownerIsSignedIn` is rechecked after each wait in `reconcile(key:)`, `applyCloudList`, `mergeCloudPositions`, and before every cloud write in `push`. ## francoisgn · 2026-11-07T13:08:54Z

## Child report ext/francoisgn-bug-566

**PR #575**: `Closes #566`. Head `bcc13106a59a50c1e806daf6fa06de7e3b1647d3`. Ready, labelled `codeql`.
- **CI** 🟢 on the head: App build + tests (iOS 27 simulator), CodeQL Swift, SwiftLint, gitleaks.
- **CodeRabbit**: 2 rounds, 3 threads fixed. A 4th thread (Major) came after the round limit; it is resolved but left for Fable to judge (see "For the Fable review").

### Root cause
Confirmed at `81d78b3`; the code on this path has not changed since d4b15a8.
- 🔴 **H1**
  - `onDisappear` calls `stopLoading()`, which aborts the page navigation before `ready`.
  - When the same view comes back, `show()` skips the document because the token is unchanged, and `onAppear()` returns because `page != nil`.
  - The spinner then stays until "Actualiser". `FilteredPostsView` has the same pattern.
- 🔴 **H1b**
  - The model is shared through `NavigationModel.reader(for:)`.
  - A replacement view B appears while A's load runs and simply joins it. Then A's `onDisappear` calls `cancel()`.
  - The model is left with no page, no load and no error. The old test `aCancelledLoadNeverDelivers` asserted this end state.
- 🟠 **H2**: there was no `didFail` / `didFailProvisionalNavigation` handler.
- 🟠 **H3**: an unrequested `.failure(.cancelled)` hit a `break`, which left the model idle.

The Mac trace will show which of H1 and H1b fires on the device. Both are fixed.

### Fix
- **`ReaderWebController`**
  - `stopLoading()` forgets a document that never became ready, so the next `show()` loads it.
  - It tracks the page `WKNavigation`. A failure of that navigation triggers one reload; a second failure is reported through `Events.failed`.
- **Views**: both reader views add `.onAppear { show }` and show a retry instead of the spinner when the page fails.
- **Model**
  - Only the view that appeared last can cancel a load (`viewer` id).
  - An unrequested `.cancelled` is fetched again once; a second one becomes `.other`.
  - `documentDidFail` sets `.other` and resets `readyToken`. That error is cleared only by the same token becoming ready; a new load takes over the error.
  - #108 still holds: a cancelled or superseded load never delivers.
- **`ReaderLog`**: `#if DEBUG` only, logs numbers and tokens only.

### Tests and how they fail on the old code
The tests come first, in commit 91b80b9. I did not run that commit alone on CI: drafts skip the macOS jobs (#477).
- **`ReaderInsetsWebTests.aDocumentStoppedBeforeItWasReadyLoadsAgainWhenShownAgain`** (real `WKWebView`): `show(1)`, then `stopLoading()`, then `show(1)`; it expects `ready == [1]`.
  - Old code: the second `show` returns at the token guard, so the test times out.
  - This assumes `stopLoading()` aborts a navigation started in the same main-actor turn.
- **`TopicReaderModelLifecycleTests`**
  - `anUnrequestedCancellationIsAskedAgainOnce` and `aRepeatedUnrequestedCancellationIsAnError`: on the old code the model stays idle (`page == nil`, `error == nil`, one request).
  - `anOldViewGoingAwayDoesNotStopTheLoadOfTheViewThatReplacedIt` (H1b): uses the new API. The old equivalent (two `onAppear`, then `cancel()`) ends idle.
  - `aDocumentTheWebViewCouldNotShowIsAnError` (H2): uses the new API; the old code had no path from a navigation failure to an error.
  - `aFailedDocumentShownAfterAllClearsItsErrorOnly`, `aLaterFetchFailureSurvivesTheOldDocumentBecomingReady` and `aFailureOfADocumentReadyEarlierIsStillAnError`: these cover the CodeRabbit fixes.
  - `theCurrentViewGoingAwayStopsTheLoadAndItsReturnLoadsAgain`: guards #108.

### Mac check needed
Use a debug build with the console filter `subsystem:SwiftHFR category:reader`.
1. **Apple topic** "Super HFR+ l'app du forum pour iOS": open it from Catégories (3 columns), then from Favoris, then open an MP. None of them may stay on the spinner. In the trace, look for `stopLoading … ready=false` followed by `show loads` for the same token, and for `disappear … current=false`.
2. **Large topic with images set to "never"**: switch columns or tabs while it loads. It must still end on the page.
3. **Link-pushed reader**: follow a link, then go Back. The page must be as it was left, with no reload (`show skipped … ready=true`).
4. **Failures**: report any `navigation failed` line with its `code`.
5. **After #540 merges**: run the checks again (it changes the compact-width lifecycle).

### For the Fable review
CodeRabbit's last thread, deferred: while two views overlap, a late `ready` from the old controller could clear the new controller's failure. The fix it proposes is to tag ready and failed events with the controller's identity. I judged this narrow, but it is plausible.

Stopping here: no merge. I wait for the Fable findings.

## Child report ext/francoisgn-composer-b

- **Issue**: #599 (part of #592, umbrella #414)
- **PR**: #602, `fix/599-composer-bar-lock`, head `e319361`, ready, labelled `codeql`. Not merged.

### Status
- 🟢 CI is green: the app build and tests on the iOS 27 simulator, SwiftLint, HFRKit on Linux, the CodeQL Swift and Actions scans, gitleaks, pre-commit and the docs check.
- 🟢 CodeRabbit raised 1 thread, a nitpick asking for a test through the composer view. I declined it with reasons: `@FocusState` cannot be driven from a unit test, and there is no UI-test target. The thread is resolved and 0 threads are open.
- Next step: the Fable review.

### What changed
- New `ComposerBarFocus.swift`, a pure rule: the bar is off while the recipient or subject field has focus, and a bar action never takes focus from either of them.
- The bar moves from a `VStack` child to `.safeAreaInset(edge: .bottom)`. I chose that over the `.keyboard` placement because the keyboard placement is rebuilt for each focused field, shows above all three fields, and disappears without an on-screen keyboard.
- `FormattingToolbar.swift` is not touched; #598 owns it.

### For the reviewer and the operator
- 🟠 **Deliberate deviation from the brief**: with no field focused, the bar stays on. That keeps the reply composer unchanged: a reply whose sheet closed without inserting anything keeps a working bar. Tell me if you want the strict reading, where the bar is on only while the text has focus.
- 🟠 **Open point for the device**: if the recipient → subject step still jumps, the likely cause is the keyboard's own height. The recipient field has `autocorrectionDisabled`, so it probably shows no predictive bar, and the keyboard grows when focus reaches the subject. Changing that means changing the fields' keyboard settings, which is the operator's call.
- The Mac check list is in the PR: new MP on iPhone, reply unchanged, a sheet closed without inserting, an iPad with a hardware keyboard, and banners appearing.

| #2 Cover screen: Actualiser and Retour at the bottom of the right column | ea531a2a | HFR-099 |

- **Root cause of #3**: the iOS 27 large-title collapse → push layout loop (`_observeScrollViewDidScroll` / `_updateSafeAreaInsets`), the one already worked around on the inner display in e1fdb57. That workaround only applied to regular/regular, so the cover screen kept large titles. Inline titles now apply on both Duo displays (`PhoneDisplayPolicy`). The cover screen is recognised three ways: the model identifier `iPhone19,4`, the side safe-area strip in portrait, or an unfold seen since launch.
- **Not built**: the cloud has no Xcode. What was checked:
  - `swiftc -parse` on every touched Swift file;
  - `swiftlint` on the new files;
  - the new pure-logic file compiled and run on Linux.
- **Review**: Sonnet adversarial review, 3 rounds.
  - Round 1: 11 findings. Fixed: detection that relied on one signal, a flag persisted into backups, rotation inconsistency, toolbar grouping, swipe-guard reset. Left as device checks: two speculative findings about the bottom bars.

  - Round 3: no blocking findings.
- **PR body changelog** updated, and the report is posted on #372: https://github.com/ezzz/HFRnow/pull/372#issuecomment-6734021916

- #3 → **#681**, new: `[SwiftHFR]`, labelled `type: bug`, `priority: high`, `status: ready`, `host: mac`, `beta-test finding`; M4 in the title.
- #1 → duplicate **#672**. I commented there with the legacy pointer, the SwiftHFR counterpart and a label proposal (M4, `host: mac`). I did not relabel it, because it is not my issue.

### SwiftHFR PRs
None.
- #681: SwiftHFR has the same precondition (`ForumListView` uses an automatic large title in the collapsed split of `CategoriesTabView`). But the fix needs either Duo detection or inline titles on every iPhone, and that is a design decision, not a small fix.
- #672: needs JS, bridge, `PageSwipe` and tests. That is `difficulty: medium`.

Both are left `ready`.

### What the operator must check on the Duo
These checks need the **next build**; the version/build bump is your call.

   - scroll Catégories to « Discussions », then open 5 categories;
   - repeat in Favoris, Messages and Plus;

## francoisgn · 2026-11-10T06:07:30Z

## Child report cloud/i704-sonnet

**Status: blocked, no PR opened, nothing pushed.** Issue #704.

**What I checked**
- `MessageTitleToneTests` (App/SwiftHFRTests/Messages/MessageTitleToneTests.swift) already renders synchronously with `ImageRenderer` and samples one pixel. There is no timer, run-loop spin, `Task.sleep` or polling loop to replace.
- Job 129376347176 (run 42681255988, attempt 1): `drawnColour(dark:)` is logged as `failed` at 63.372 s. In the same log, every test of that stretch reports 63–72 s (e.g. `toneFollowsTheMembersReadState` 63.453 s, `PollModelTests` 72 s). So the 63 s is the wait of a queued `@MainActor` test, not time spent in the test itself.
- The CI log carries no assertion text (no `Expectation failed`, no file:line). The failure is only the `failed` line, so I cannot tell which `#expect` or `#require` fired (a nil render, a sampled colour more than 0.02 off, or one of the two swatches).
- Only `drawnColour` and `rowDrawsTheTitleTone` compare a rendered pixel with a dynamic UIColor (`.label`, `ReadTitleStyle`) resolved by traits. Those are the two failing tests.

**Hypothesis (unproven):** the dynamic colours are resolved against ambient appearance state while other main-actor suites change it. `AppThemeTests` and `AppThemeStabilityTests` set the host window's `overrideUserInterfaceStyle`, and `.serialized` only orders tests inside each suite. I would not change the test on this guess alone.

**What a Mac run should measure** (to confirm before a fix)
1. Run `MessageTitleToneTests` alone 20 times, then together with `AppThemeTests` (same process, simulator clone), and see whether `drawnColour` fails only in the second case.
2. On failure, print the sampled RGB and the expected RGB (add temporary `Comment` text to the `#expect`s). If the sampled colour is the other scheme's, the ambient-appearance hypothesis holds. The fix would then be `.serialized` across the appearance-mutating suites, or reading the colour from a resolved `UITraitCollection` instead of the render. If the sampled colour is the background, `ImageRenderer` returned an unrendered frame under load, and the fix is a retry-free re-render check.
3. If the Mac run is clean, keep the new failure's `xcresult` from CI: its issue text names the failing expectation.

Not claimed or touched: nothing besides #704. Branch `fix/flaky-title-tone-test` exists locally only, with no commits.
